# Source Integration Manifest

Every incoming non-research input has an explicit decision. Research hashes are in `📓️research-intake.md`. Snapshot build output is excluded. Concurrent semantic renames were retained and mapped by bytes or stable identifiers. Identical assets may share one destination.
